6755    /// The path calls a per-process file's owner makes, measured on Linux 7.1
6756    /// against /proc/self/cgroup under Hermit's ptrace backend (every
6757    /// capability) and natively (none).
6758    #[test]
6759    fn path_calls_follow_linux_for_a_process_file_its_owner_names() {
6760        for (capabilities, writes, xattr, security) in [
6761            (
6762                ALL_CAPABILITIES,
6763                Ok(0),
6764                Errno::EOPNOTSUPP,
6765                Errno::EOPNOTSUPP,
6766            ),
6767            (0, Err(Errno::EACCES), Errno::EACCES, Errno::EPERM),
6768        ] {
6769            let access = FileAccess::owned_with(capabilities);
6770            let answer = |request| path_request_answer(request, None, None, access);
6771            let probe = |mode: i32| {
6772                answer(PathRequest::Access {
6773                    mode: mode as u64,
6774                    flags: 0,
6775                })
6776            };
6777            assert_eq!(probe(libc::R_OK), Ok(0));
6778            assert_eq!(probe(libc::W_OK), writes);
6779            assert_eq!(probe(libc::X_OK), Err(Errno::EACCES));
6780            assert_eq!(answer(PathRequest::Truncate { length: 0 }), writes);
6781            assert_eq!(
6782                answer(PathRequest::Truncate { length: -1 }),
6783                Err(Errno::EINVAL)
6784            );
6785            assert_eq!(answer(PathRequest::Chmod { flags: 0 }), Err(Errno::EPERM));
6786            for explicit in [None, Some(false), Some(true)] {
6787                assert_eq!(answer(PathRequest::Times { explicit }), Ok(0));
6788            }
6789            let change = |name: &[u8]| {
6790                path_request_answer(
6791                    PathRequest::ChangeXattr {
6792                        size: Some(1),
6793                        value: 0,
6794                        flags: 0,
6795                    },
6796                    Some(name),
6797                    Some(&b"1"[..]),
6798                    access,
6799                )
6800            };
6801            assert_eq!(change(b"user.x"), Err(xattr));
6802            assert_eq!(change(b"security.x"), Err(security));
6803            assert_eq!(change(b"trusted.x"), Err(Errno::EPERM));
6804            assert_eq!(
6805                xattr_change_answer(b"user.x", None, None, access),
6806                Err(xattr)
6807            );
6808        }
6809    }
6810
6811    /// A per-process file has no `mmap` method: a mapping is `ENODEV`, as for
6812    /// a sysctl file, where a system-wide `seq_file` is `EIO`; and a
6813    /// descriptor opened for writing may be mapped shared and writable up to
6814    /// that answer, but one that cannot read may not be mapped at all.
6815    #[test]
6816    fn a_process_file_maps_as_a_file_without_mmap() {
6817        let read = ProtFlags::PROT_READ;
6818        let write = ProtFlags::PROT_READ | ProtFlags::PROT_WRITE;
6819        let check = |prot, flags, seq_file, mode| {
6820            rendered_mmap_file_checks(4096, 0, prot, flags, false, seq_file, mode)
6821        };
6822        let private = MapFlags::MAP_PRIVATE;
6823        let shared = MapFlags::MAP_SHARED;
6824        assert_eq!(check(read, private, false, (true, false)), Errno::ENODEV);
6825        assert_eq!(check(write, shared, false, (true, true)), Errno::ENODEV);
6826        assert_eq!(check(write, shared, false, (true, false)), Errno::EACCES);
6827        assert_eq!(check(read, private, false, (false, true)), Errno::EACCES);
6828        assert_eq!(check(read, shared, false, (false, true)), Errno::EACCES);
6829        assert_eq!(
6830            check(read, private | MapFlags::MAP_GROWSDOWN, false, (true, true)),
6831            Errno::ENODEV
6832        );
6833        assert_eq!(check(read, private, true, (true, false)), Errno::EIO);
6834    }
6835
6836    /// Detcore's process table decides whether a per-process file's task
6837    /// exists: a listed process (a zombie too), a live thread of a listed
6838    /// process below its `task`, and the namespace's init with its one thread.
6839    #[test]
6840    fn a_process_file_exists_for_exactly_the_tasks_the_table_lists() {
6841        use crate::procfs::BoundTask;
6842        use crate::procfs::listing::ListingFacts;
6843        let facts = ListingFacts {
6844            processes: vec![1, 3, 7],
6845            threads: vec![3, 5],
6846        };
6847        let task = |process, thread| BoundTask { process, thread };
6848        assert!(crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6849            task(3, None),
6850            Some(3),
6851            &facts
6852        ));
6853        assert!(crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6854            task(3, Some(5)),
6855            Some(3),
6856            &facts
6857        ));
6858        assert!(!crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6859            task(3, Some(6)),
6860            Some(3),
6861            &facts
6862        ));
6863        // A live non-leader thread's own directory, in its group.
6864        assert!(crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6865            task(5, None),
6866            Some(3),
6867            &facts
6868        ));
6869        assert!(crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6870            task(5, Some(3)),
6871            Some(3),
6872            &facts
6873        ));
6874        // A reaped process (its leader is no longer listed), or an id that
6875        // names no task.
6876        assert!(!crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6877            task(9, None),
6878            Some(9),
6879            &facts
6880        ));
6881        assert!(!crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6882            task(11, None),
6883            None,
6884            &facts
6885        ));
6886        // Hermit's own init, when no guest holds pid 1.
6887        let without_init = ListingFacts {
6888            processes: vec![3],
6889            threads: Vec::new(),
6890        };
6891        assert!(crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6892            task(1, None),
6893            None,
6894            &without_init
6895        ));
6896        assert!(crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6897            task(1, Some(1)),
6898            None,
6899            &without_init
6900        ));
6901        assert!(!crate::procfs::listing::task_exists(
6902            task(1, Some(2)),
6903            None,
6904            &without_init
6905        ));
6906    }
6907
6908    /// A process's `auxv` is mode 0400, readable by its owner only, measured
6909    /// natively on Linux 7.1: its owner opens it to read; a zombie's (global
6910    /// root's) cannot be opened, `access(R_OK)` and a `user.` attribute read
6911    /// are EACCES, a `security.` one is the file system's EOPNOTSUPP, and a
6912    /// `trusted.` one is invisible (ENODATA); with CAP_DAC_OVERRIDE its owner
6913    /// may open it to write.
6914    #[test]
6915    fn a_process_auxv_is_readable_by_its_owner_only() {
6916        let mut file = crate::procfs::ProcfsFile::from_path(Path::new("/proc/self/auxv")).unwrap();
6917        file.bind_task(3, 3);
6918        let path = file.into_rendered().opened_path().unwrap();
6919        assert!(rendered_path_is_owner_only(path));
6920        let mut cgroup =
6921            crate::procfs::ProcfsFile::from_path(Path::new("/proc/self/cgroup")).unwrap();
6922        cgroup.bind_task(3, 3);
6923        assert!(!rendered_path_is_owner_only(
6924            cgroup.into_rendered().opened_path().unwrap()
6925        ));
6926        assert!(!rendered_path_is_owner_only(Path::new("/proc/stat")));
6927        let epoch = Timespec {
6928            tv_sec: 0,
6929            tv_nsec: 0,
6930        };
6931        assert_eq!(
6932            rendered_stat(path, epoch, (0, 0)).mode,
6933            libc::S_IFREG | 0o400
6934        );
6935        let owner = FileAccess::owned_with(ALL_CAPABILITIES);
6936        assert_eq!(
6937            rendered_open_flags(OFlag::O_RDONLY, owner),
6938            Ok(OFlag::O_RDONLY)
6939        );
6940        assert_eq!(rendered_open_flags(OFlag::O_RDWR, owner), Ok(OFlag::O_RDWR));
6941        let zombie = FileAccess {
6942            readable: false,
6943            ..FileAccess::GLOBAL_ROOT
6944        };
6945        for flags in [
6946            OFlag::O_RDONLY,
6947            OFlag::O_WRONLY,
6948            OFlag::O_RDWR,
6949            OFlag::O_ACCMODE,
6950        ] {
6951            assert_eq!(rendered_open_flags(flags, zombie), Err(Errno::EACCES));
6952        }
6953        // An `O_PATH` descriptor asks for no access.
6954        assert_eq!(
6955            rendered_open_flags(OFlag::O_PATH, zombie),
6956            Ok(OFlag::O_PATH)
6957        );
6958        let probe = |mode: i32| {
6959            path_request_answer(
6960                PathRequest::Access {
6961                    mode: mode as u64,
6962                    flags: 0,
6963                },
6964                None,
6965                None,
6966                zombie,
6967            )
6968        };
6969        assert_eq!(probe(libc::F_OK), Ok(0));
6970        assert_eq!(probe(libc::R_OK), Err(Errno::EACCES));
6971        let get =
6972            |name: &[u8]| path_request_answer(PathRequest::GetXattr, Some(name), None, zombie);
6973        assert_eq!(get(b"user.x"), Err(Errno::EACCES));
6974        assert_eq!(get(b"security.x"), Err(Errno::EOPNOTSUPP));
6975        assert_eq!(get(b"trusted.x"), Err(Errno::ENODATA));
6976    }
6977
6978    /// What decides another process's open of a rendered `auxv`: the
6979    /// target's probe opens (allowed); it is refused while the caller's own
6980    /// `/proc/thread-self/stat` opens (the kernel beneath decided: its errno);
6981    /// that is refused with EACCES too (no `/proc` beneath: the caller's
6982    /// `CAP_SYS_PTRACE` decides); any other errno is the open's answer.
6983    #[test]
6984    fn another_process_opens_auxv_as_the_kernel_beneath_answers() {
6985        use AuxvAccess::Answer;
6986        use AuxvAccess::CallerCapability;
6987        for serves in [Ok(()), Err(Errno::EACCES), Err(Errno::EMFILE)] {
6988            assert_eq!(auxv_access_answer(Ok(()), serves), Answer(Ok(())));
6989        }
6990        for refused in [Errno::EACCES, Errno::EPERM, Errno::ESRCH, Errno::EMFILE] {
6991            assert_eq!(
6992                auxv_access_answer(Err(refused), Ok(())),
6993                Answer(Err(refused))
6994            );
6995        }
6996        assert_eq!(
6997            auxv_access_answer(Err(Errno::EACCES), Err(Errno::EACCES)),
6998            CallerCapability
6999        );
7000        for operational in [Errno::EMFILE, Errno::ENFILE, Errno::ENOMEM] {
7001            assert_eq!(
7002                auxv_access_answer(Err(operational), Err(operational)),
7003                Answer(Err(operational))
7004            );
7005            assert_eq!(
7006                auxv_access_answer(Err(Errno::EACCES), Err(operational)),
7007                Answer(Err(operational))
7008            );
7009        }
7010    }
7011
7012    /// A per-process file belongs to the guest's own identity, and the path
7013    /// calls Detcore resolves itself include its name.
7014    #[test]
7015    fn a_process_file_is_owned_by_the_guest_and_named_by_path_calls() {
7016        assert_eq!(rendered_owner(Path::new("/proc/3/cgroup")), Ok((0, 0)));
7017        assert_eq!(
7018            rendered_owner(Path::new("/proc/3/task/5/cgroup")),
7019            Ok((0, 0))
7020        );
7021        let stat = rendered_stat(
7022            Path::new("/proc/3/cgroup"),
7023            Timespec {
7024                tv_sec: 0,
7025                tv_nsec: 0,
7026            },
7027            (0, 0),
7028        );
7029        assert_eq!(stat.mode, libc::S_IFREG | 0o444);
7030        assert_eq!(
7031            stat.inode,
7032            procfs_inode::rendered_inode(Path::new("/proc/3/cgroup"))
7033        );
7034        assert_ne!(
7035            stat.inode,
7036            procfs_inode::rendered_inode(Path::new("/proc/3/task/3/cgroup"))
7037        );
7038        assert!(could_name_rendered(
7039            Path::new("/proc/self/cgroup"),
7040            RenderedFiles::WITHOUT_CPUINFO,
7041            false
7042        ));
7043        assert!(could_name_rendered(
7044            Path::new("cgroup"),
7045            RenderedFiles::WITHOUT_CPUINFO,
7046            false
7047        ));
7048    }
7049
7050    /// A rendered `comm` (package K slice A+) has Linux's mode, 0644, at its
7051    /// task's path, and Detcore never opens one for writing: an open that
7052    /// asks to write (a write or read-write access mode, or `O_TRUNC`) is
7053    /// one it refuses (`asks_to_write_comm`). A read-only or `O_PATH` open
7054    /// is not, and a write open of any other rendered file stays Detcore's
7055    /// own access check's. Where the kernel serves every `comm`, a spelling ending in
7056    /// `comm` is not one Detcore resolves.
7057    #[test]
7058    fn a_rendered_comm_is_0644_and_detcore_opens_it_only_for_reading() {
7059        let bound = |spelling: &str| {
7060            let mut file = ProcfsFile::from_path(Path::new(spelling)).unwrap();
7061            file.bind_task(3, 5);
7062            file
7063        };
7064        let comm = bound("/proc/thread-self/comm");
7065        let path = comm.rendered_path(RenderedFiles::ALL).unwrap();
7066        assert_eq!(path, Path::new("/proc/3/task/5/comm"));
7067        let epoch = Timespec {
7068            tv_sec: 0,
7069            tv_nsec: 0,
7070        };
7071        assert_eq!(
7072            rendered_stat(path, epoch, (0, 0)).mode,
7073            libc::S_IFREG | 0o644
7074        );
7075        let stat = bound("/proc/thread-self/stat");
7076        let stat_path = stat.rendered_path(RenderedFiles::ALL).unwrap();
7077        assert_eq!(
7078            rendered_stat(stat_path, epoch, (0, 0)).mode,
7079            libc::S_IFREG | 0o444
7080        );
7081        for flags in [
7082            OFlag::O_WRONLY,
7083            OFlag::O_RDWR,
7084            OFlag::O_RDWR | OFlag::O_CLOEXEC,
7085            OFlag::O_ACCMODE,
7086            OFlag::O_RDONLY | OFlag::O_TRUNC,
7087            OFlag::O_WRONLY | OFlag::O_APPEND,
7088        ] {
7089            assert!(asks_to_write_comm(&comm, flags), "{flags:?}");
7090            assert!(!asks_to_write_comm(&stat, flags), "{flags:?}");
7091        }
7092        for flags in [
7093            OFlag::O_RDONLY,
7094            OFlag::O_RDONLY | OFlag::O_CLOEXEC | OFlag::O_NONBLOCK,
7095            OFlag::O_PATH,
7096            OFlag::O_PATH | OFlag::O_CLOEXEC,
7097        ] {
7098            assert!(!asks_to_write_comm(&comm, flags), "{flags:?}");
7099        }
7100        for spelling in ["/proc/self/comm", "comm", "/proc/3/task/5/comm"] {
7101            assert!(
7102                could_name_rendered(Path::new(spelling), RenderedFiles::ALL, true),
7103                "{spelling}"
7104            );
7105            assert!(
7106                !could_name_rendered(Path::new(spelling), RenderedFiles::ALL, false),
7107                "{spelling}"
7108            );
7109        }
7110        assert!(could_name_rendered(
7111            Path::new("/proc/self/stat"),
7112            RenderedFiles::ALL,
7113            false
7114        ));
7115    }
7116
7117    /// Detcore refuses an open of a rendered `comm` for writing itself
7118    /// (package K slice A+): EACCES, with no injected call and no request to
7119    /// the global tool (the guest records every injection and panics on any
7120    /// request), by its path and through a descriptor link, for a write or
7121    /// read-write access mode, `O_TRUNC`, and `creat`'s flags. So the answer
7122    /// depends on neither the host nor the kernel beneath.
7123    #[test]
7124    fn a_write_open_of_a_rendered_comm_is_refused_with_nothing_injected() {
7125        use crate::record_or_replay::NoopTool;
7126        use crate::syscall_hot_path_tests::InjectGuest;
7127        use crate::syscall_hot_path_tests::detcore;
7128        futures::executor::block_on(async {
7129            let tool = detcore::<NoopTool>();
7130            for spelling in ["/proc/self/comm", "/proc/thread-self/comm", "/proc/3/comm"] {
7131                for via_link in [false, true] {
7132                    for flags in [
7133                        OFlag::O_WRONLY,
7134                        OFlag::O_RDWR,
7135                        OFlag::O_RDWR | OFlag::O_CLOEXEC,
7136                        OFlag::O_RDONLY | OFlag::O_TRUNC,
7137                        OFlag::O_WRONLY | OFlag::O_CREAT | OFlag::O_TRUNC,
7138                        OFlag::O_ACCMODE,
7139                    ] {
7140                        let mut procfs = ProcfsFile::from_path(Path::new(spelling)).unwrap();
7141                        procfs.bind_task(3, 5);
7142                        let target = RenderedTarget { procfs, via_link };
7143                        let mut guest = InjectGuest::<NoopTool>::new(Ok(0));
7144                        let answer = tool
7145                            .open_rendered_procfs(&mut guest, flags, target)
7146                            .await
7147                            .map_err(|error| error.into_errno().unwrap());
7148                        assert_eq!(
7149                            answer,
7150                            Err(Errno::EACCES),
7151                            "{spelling} {flags:?} via_link={via_link}"
7152                        );
7153                        assert!(
7154                            guest.injected().is_empty(),
7155                            "{spelling} {flags:?}: {:?}",
7156                            guest.injected()
7157                        );
7158                    }
7159                }
7160            }
7161        })
7162    }
7163
7164    /// Detcore's walk of a `..` spelling ending in `comm` (package K slice
7165    /// A+) follows Linux's path walk through the procfs directories it
7166    /// models: `self` and `thread-self` are links it follows, `..` goes to the
7167    /// parent, every task a component names is a check in path order, a name
7168    /// below `task` that is no number is ENOENT, a component after `comm` is
7169    /// ENOTDIR, and anything else it does not model (a link such as `cwd`, a
7170    /// directory such as `attr`, a step out of `/proc`) is undetermined.
7171    #[test]
7172    fn a_comm_alias_walk_follows_linuxs_lookup_through_what_detcore_models() {
7173        use crate::procfs::BoundTask;
7174        let own = (3, 5);
7175        let task = |process, thread| BoundTask { process, thread };
7176        let comm = |checks: Vec<BoundTask>, process, thread| CommAliasWalk::Comm {
7177            checks,
7178            task: task(process, thread),
7179        };
7180        let fails = |checks: Vec<BoundTask>, errno| CommAliasWalk::Fails { checks, errno };
7181        for (path, expected) in [
7182            (
7183                "/proc/self/task/5/../5/comm",
7184                comm(vec![task(3, Some(5)), task(3, Some(5))], 3, Some(5)),
7185            ),
7186            (
7187                "/proc/self/task/9/../5/comm",
7188                comm(vec![task(3, Some(9)), task(3, Some(5))], 3, Some(5)),
7189            ),
7190            (
7191                "/proc/thread-self/../5/comm",
7192                comm(vec![task(3, Some(5))], 3, Some(5)),
7193            ),
7194            ("/proc/thread-self/../../comm", comm(vec![], 3, None)),
7195            ("/proc/self/../3/comm", comm(vec![task(3, None)], 3, None)),
7196            ("/proc/../proc/self/comm", comm(vec![], 3, None)),
7197            ("/proc/./self/task/../comm", comm(vec![], 3, None)),
7198            (
7199                "/proc/7/../7/task/8/comm",
7200                comm(
7201                    vec![task(7, None), task(7, None), task(7, Some(8))],
7202                    7,
7203                    Some(8),
7204                ),
7205            ),
7206            ("/proc/self/comm/../comm", fails(vec![], Errno::ENOTDIR)),
7207            ("/proc/self/task/x/../5/comm", fails(vec![], Errno::ENOENT)),
7208            ("/proc/self/task/05/../5/comm", fails(vec![], Errno::ENOENT)),
7209            ("/proc/007/../self/comm", fails(vec![], Errno::ENOENT)),
7210            ("/proc/self/task/comm/../comm", fails(vec![], Errno::ENOENT)),
7211            (
7212                "/proc/self/attr/../comm",
7213                CommAliasWalk::Undetermined(vec![]),
7214            ),
7215            (
7216                "/proc/self/cwd/../comm",
7217                CommAliasWalk::Undetermined(vec![]),
7218            ),
7219            (
7220                "/proc/7/fd/../comm",
7221                CommAliasWalk::Undetermined(vec![task(7, None)]),
7222            ),
7223            ("/proc/../etc/comm", CommAliasWalk::Undetermined(vec![])),
7224            ("/proc/self/comm", CommAliasWalk::NotApplicable),
7225            ("/proc/self/task/../stat", CommAliasWalk::NotApplicable),
7226            ("/tmp/../proc/self/comm", CommAliasWalk::NotApplicable),
7227            ("proc/self/../self/comm", CommAliasWalk::NotApplicable),
7228        ] {
7229            assert_eq!(walk_comm_alias(Path::new(path), own), expected, "{path}");
7230        }
7231    }
